Most of the +4.37pp ΔF1 vs Qwen greedy comes from LLaMA being stronger (greedy 0.784 vs 0.748), but the +0.42pp vs pure LLaMA is genuine cross-model diversity gain. Supports diversity hypothesis: different models make complementary errors → better consensus.\"}, \"exp_b1_scd\": {\"status\": \"PENDING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T11:12:48.805Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T11:12:56.838Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). D176: appendix-only, not main claim.\"}, \"exp_pareto_diversity_quality\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:31:45.421Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:31:58.776Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"There exists a Pareto frontier in the diversity (1-degeneracy) vs construction quality (ΔF1) space across temperature configurations. Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\", \"metrics\": {\"dataset\": \"SciERC\", \"configs\": {\"t07\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.777, \"uniform_delta\": 1.81, \"vc_delta\": 1.17, \"pareto\": \"uniform only\"}, \"t08\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.819, \"uniform_delta\": 1.25, \"vc_delta\": 1.68, \"pareto\": \"VC only\"}, \"mix_07_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.83, \"uniform_delta\": 1.43, \"vc_delta\": 1.67, \"pareto\": \"both\"}, \"t10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.877, \"uniform_delta\": 0.75, \"vc_delta\": 1.21, \"pareto\": \"both (highest diversity)\"}, \"mix_08_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.789, \"uniform_delta\": -0.17, \"vc_delta\": 0.47, \"pareto\": \"dominated\"}}, \"vc_slope_reduction\": \"~50% flatter than uniform\", \"key_insight\": \"well-separated T mixing dual Pareto optimal; close T mixing dominated\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"A4 Pareto diversity-quality analysis on SciERC. Mix T=0.7+T=1.0 is dual-Pareto optimal (uniform +1.43pp, VC +1.67pp). VC construction flattens trade-off slope ~50% vs uniform — increasing diversity costs less quality. Mix T=0.8+T=1.0 is dominated (close T → higher degeneracy, no construction gain). Confirms DGEC heterogeneous-T finding: ΔT matters, ΔT=0.3 beneficial, ΔT=0.2 harmful. Pareto plot artifact registered.\"}, \"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:22.761Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:41.158Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\", \"metrics\": {\"model\": \"Qwen3-8B FT\", \"fewnerd\": {\"n_instances\": 37648, \"greedy_f1\": 0.7884, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.7819, \"best_adaptive\": \"h1_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.7849, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"+0.30pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"43.8%\", \"note\": \"Greedy > U8; adaptive h1/l8 best: greedy for high-conf, N=8 SC for low-conf\"}, \"scierc\": {\"n_instances\": 551, \"greedy_f1\": 0.6592, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.6712, \"best_adaptive\": \"top67%_h2_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.6707, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"-0.05pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"50.2%\", \"note\": \"U8 > Greedy; top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with negligible loss\"}}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"B7 adaptive sampling budget analysis on Qwen3-8B FT. Key findings: (1) FewNERD: greedy already beats uniform N=8 SC (+0.65pp), adaptive h1/l8 is optimal (+0.30pp vs U8, saves 43.8% samples); (2) SciERC: U8 beats greedy (+1.20pp), top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with only -0.05pp loss. Practical implication: confidence-gated sampling can halve inference cost. Artifacts: vc_8b_qwen3_ft_results, sj_8b_qwen3_ft_results (registered by worker).\"}, \"exp_dgec_sj_construction\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T08:37:58.013Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:26:30.567Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"SJ-weighted entity construction serves as control for VC — if SJ (soft entity overlap) works as well as VC (exact match consensus), any stronger QE signal suffices for construction; if VC specifically outperforms SJ, exact-match consensus has a unique advantage over soft similarity for entity-level selection.\", \"metrics\": {\"total_configs\": 26, \"significant_positive\": 1, \"significant_negative\": 15, \"not_significant\": 10, \"best_config\": \"scierc/72b/zs +2.0pp p=0.001\", \"worst_config\": \"fewnerd/8b_qwen3/ft mean -1.48pp all seeds p=1.0\", \"qwen3_8b_ft_supplement\": {\"configs\": 9, \"sig+\": 0, \"sig-\": 6, \"ns\": 3, \"fewnerd_mean_delta\": -1.48, \"scierc_mean_delta\": -0.4, \"conll_mean_delta\": -0.5, \"note\": \"SJ fully negative on Qwen3-8B FT — consistent with Qwen2.5-7B FT pattern\"}, \"m030_all_passed\": true}, \"notes\": \"COMPLETE (26 configs). SJ-weighted construction: 1 sig+ / 15 sig- / 10 ns. Qwen3-8B FT supplement (9 configs): 0+/6-/3ns — fully negative, consistent with 7B FT pattern. FewNERD -1.48pp mean (all seeds p=1.0). CoNLL all negative (high degeneracy). WNUT17/SciERC lean positive but not robust. Key answer: VC does NOT reliably improve where LP already works — seed-dependent, dataset-dependent. Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: Structurally diverse prompt templates (role framing, output format variants, few-shot exemplar diversity) increase inter-sample entity extraction diversity beyond temperature-only diversity, improving construction F1 over identical-prompt N=8 sampling on FewNERD/SciERC/CoNLL.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_asb_adaptive_budget: Adaptive Sample Budget: extension of E_θ threshold optimization to sample count N. Instance features (degeneracy, LP variance, agreement rate) predict optimal N per instance. Under fixed total budget=8 (average), adaptive allocation outperforms uniform N=8 by allocating more samples to high-uncertainty instances and fewer to degenerate/easy ones.（排队等待 GPU）\\n- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\\n- exp_backup1_het_temp: Heterogeneous-Temperature Sampling: MRSC/LSC negative results prove entity-level correction cannot close the selection gap. F4 degeneracy analysis shows T=0.7 has ~62% degeneracy (too homogeneous) while T=1.0 has more diversity but noisier entities. Mixing samples from different temperatures (k from T_low + (8-k) from T_high) breaks temperature-specific degeneracy patterns: low-T samples provide precise entities, high-T samples provide diversity. Mixed-T N=8 pool should achieve lower degeneracy than pure T_low and higher construction F1 than pure T_high. Hypothesis source\\n\\n... [Director 指令] [Director 指令 — 三张卡立即分配]

**1. Kill B3 prompted diversity。** 旧 negative 无新 hypothesis，不重跑。如已 spawn worker 立即停止。

**2. cuda:1 → Backup 3: Distillation-based Verifier（新实验，立即起草 spec）**

设计要点：
- **目标**：训练 supervised entity-level classifier 预测 entity 是否在 gold set 中，用分类分数做 weighted aggregation，替代 MV 的 frequency voting
- **输入特征（per entity）**：LP score、vote count (agreement ratio)、degeneracy flag、entity span length、entity type 分布、N (sample count)
- **标签**：binary（entity in gold = 1, not in gold = 0）
- **模型**：LogisticRegression + RandomForest + small MLP（三个对比）
- **评估**：**leave-one-dataset-out CV**（train on 2 datasets, test on held-out dataset）。CoNLL/FewNERD/SciERC 三折。这解决 reviewer 公平性质疑
- **指标**：entity-level AUROC + 用 classifier score 做 weighted MV 后的 downstream F1 vs 标准 MV F1
- **数据来源**：复用已有 SCS 实验的 samples + gold labels（N=8, T=1.0, seed=42 的 configs）
- **GPU 需求**：1 GPU（如需 forward pass 提取特征）或 CPU-only（如特征已有）
- Hypothesis 来源：推导自 MRSC/ESJ/LSC 三个 negative（prompt-based self-judgment 失败 → 转 supervised signal aggregation）+ E_θ oracle 分析（oracle headroom 存在但 unsupervised 方法抓不住）

Spec + 代码 1 小时内提交审批。

**3. cuda:2 → T_low FewNERD inference（扩展 het_temp 数据覆盖）**
- 用 qwen25-7b-fewnerd-merged 在 FewNERD 上跑 T=0.5 N=8 seed=42 inference
- 目的：让 Backup 1 (het_temp) 从仅 SciERC 551 instances 扩展到 FewNERD 32565 instances

```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. I'll use leave-one-dataset-out cross-validation across CoNLL, FewNERD, and SciERC, reusing existing SCS samples for training data, and assigning it to cuda:1 on autodl-5090-c once ESJ finishes running there.
```

```text
Before spawning these workers, I need to transition Backup 3's status from PENDING to DONE since it was killed, and I also need to update the 14B CoNLL experiment status through the Director.

Now I'm handling the registry updates and then spinning up the three workers in parallel.
```

```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""B5 Cross-Model Self-Consistency: Qwen3-8B FT + LLaMA-3.1-8B FT on FewNERD.
3	
4	Compares pure-model vs mixed-model entity construction.
5	Usage:
6	    python cross_model_construction.py &lt;qwen_samples> &lt;llama_samples> &lt;output_json>
7	"""
8	
9	import json
10	import sys
11	from collections import defaultdict
12	
13	import numpy as np
14	
15	B = 10000
16	SEED = 42
17	
18	
19	def compute_entity_f1(pred_entities, gold_entities):
20	    pred = {(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"]) for e in pred_entities}
21	    gold = {(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"]) for e in gold_entities}
22	    if not gold and not pred:
23	        return 1.0
24	    if not gold or not pred:
25	        return 0.0
26	    tp = len(pred & gold)
27	    if tp == 0:
28	        return 0.0
29	    p = tp / len(pred)
30	    r = tp / len(gold)
31	    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)
32	
33	
34	def compute_vc(samples):
35	    N = len(samples)
36	    entity_sets = []
37	    for s in samples:
38	        es = frozenset((e["start"], e["end"], e["type"]) for e in s.get("entities", []))
39	        entity_sets.append(es)
40	    return [sum(1 for j in range(N) if entity_sets[j] == entity_sets[i]) / N for i in range(N)]
```

```json
      "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"B5 Cross-Model Self-Consistency: Qwen3-8B FT + LLaMA-3.1-8B FT on FewNERD.\n\nCompares pure-model vs mixed-model entity construction.\nUsage:\n    python cross_model_construction.py &lt;qwen_samples> &lt;llama_samples> &lt;output_json>\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nB = 10000\nSEED = 42\n\n\ndef compute_entity_f1(pred_entities, gold_entities):\n    pred = {(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in pred_entities}\n    gold = {(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in gold_entities}\n    if not gold and not pred:\n        return 1.0\n    if not gold or not pred:\n        return 0.0\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\n    if tp == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    p = tp / len(pred)\n    r = tp / len(gold)\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\n\n\ndef compute_vc(samples):\n    N = len(samples)\n    entity_sets = []\n    for s in samples:\n        es = frozenset((e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in s.get(\"entities\", []))\n        entity_sets.append(es)\n    return [sum(1 for j in range(N) if entity_sets[j] == entity_sets[i]) / N for i in range(N)]",
```

```text
95	    """Batch sampler backed by vLLM with optional XGrammar JSON constrained decoding."""
96	
97	    def __init__(
98	        self,
99	        model_path: str,
100	        tensor_parallel_size: int = 1,
101	        max_model_len: int = 4096,
102	        gpu_memory_utilization: float = 0.90,
103	    ):
104	        """Initialise the vLLM engine.
105	
106	        Args:
107	            model_path: Path to the HuggingFace model (local or hub id).
108	            tensor_parallel_size: Number of GPUs for tensor parallelism.
109	            max_model_len: Maximum sequence length.
110	            gpu_memory_utilization: Fraction of GPU memory to reserve.
111	        """
112	        from vllm import LLM
113	
114	        self.llm = LLM(
115	            model=model_path,
116	            tensor_parallel_size=tensor_parallel_size,
117	            max_model_len=max_model_len,
118	            gpu_memory_utilization=gpu_memory_utilization,
119	        )
120	        self._model_path = model_path
121	        self._tokenizer = None
122

124	        if self._tokenizer is None:
125	            from transformers import AutoTokenizer
126	            self._tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
127	                self._model_path, trust_remote_code=True,
128	            )
129	        return self._tokenizer
```

````json
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：设计并编写 Backup 3: Distillation-based Verifier 实验代码\n\n### 背景\nMRSC/ESJ/LSC 三个 prompt-based self-judgment 方法全部 negative，证明 unsupervised entity-level 信号无法 close selection gap。但 E_θ oracle 分析显示 oracle headroom 存在。转 supervised signal aggregation。\n\n### 实验设计（Director 指定）\n\n**目标**：训练 supervised entity-level binary classifier，预测 entity 是否在 gold set 中。用分类分数做 weighted aggregation，替代 MV 的 frequency voting。\n\n**输入特征（per entity）**：\n1. LP score（log-probability）\n2. vote count / agreement ratio（在 N 个 sample 中出现的频率）\n3. degeneracy flag（该 instance 的 N 个 sample 是否全部相同）\n4. entity span length（字符数或 token 数）\n5. entity type 分布（one-hot 或在 N samples 中的 type 一致性）\n6. N（sample count）\n\n**标签**：binary（entity in gold = 1, not in gold = 0）\n\n**模型**：LogisticRegression + RandomForest + small MLP（三个对比）\n\n**评估**：leave-one-dataset-out CV（train on 2 datasets, test on held-out dataset）\n- 三折：CoNLL held-out / FewNERD held-out / SciERC held-out\n\n**指标**：\n- entity-level AUROC\n- 用 classifier score 做 weighted MV 后的 downstream F1 vs 标准 MV F1\n\n**数据来源**：复用已有 SCS 实验的 samples + gold labels（N=8, T=1.0, seed=42 的 configs）\n\n### 你要做的\n\n1. **SSH 到 autodl-5090-c 检查可用数据**：\n   - 先用 `detect_image` 检测镜像\n   - 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/` 下有哪些 N=8 T=1.0 seed=42 的 samples.jsonl\n   - 确认三个数据集（CoNLL、FewNERD、SciERC）都有对应 samples\n   - 检查 samples.jsonl 的格式：每行应有 text、gold_entities、samples（list of N extractions）\n   - 检查每个 sample 是否包含 LP score（log_probs 字段）\n   - 如果 LP score 不在 samples 中，需要额外方案提取\n\n2. **编写主脚本** `run_distill_verifier.py`，写到本地 `artifacts/experiments/distill_verifier/run_distill_verifier.py`：\n\n   核心流程：\n   ```\n   a) load_data(): 从三个数据集的 samples.jsonl 加载数据\n   b) extract_features(): 对每个 entity 提取 6 维特征向量\n      - 遍历每个 instance 的 N 个 samples\n      - 对每个 unique entity（by start,end,type tuple），计算特征\n      - 标签：entity 是否 match gold entity（exact span+type match）\n   c) train_classifiers(): \n      - LogisticRegression (sklearn)\n      - RandomForestClassifier (sklearn, n_estimators=100)  \n      - MLPClassifier (sklearn, hidden_layer_sizes=(64,32))\n   d) leave_one_dataset_out_cv():\n      - 3 折，每次 train on 2 datasets, test on held-out\n      - 计算 AUROC per fold per model\n   e) weighted_mv_evaluation():\n      - 对每个 entity，用 classifier score 作为 weight\n      - weighted_score(entity) = classifier_prob * frequency\n      - 构建 weighted entity set：score > threshold 的 entities\n      - 对比 standard MV F1\n   f) save_results(): JSON output with all metrics\n   ```\n\n3. **代码质量要求**：\n   - 文件顶部写清目的、输入输出、依赖\n   - 每个函数写 docstring\n   - argparse 支持 --data_dir, --output_dir, --seed\n   - dry-run 验证：`python run_distill_verifier.py --help` 通过\n\n4. **确认运行命令和 spec**，用 `update_registry` 更新：\n   - exp_id: exp_backup3_distill_verifier\n   - field: spec\n   - value: {command, server, data_path, data_count, model_name: \"LR+RF+MLP ensemble\", seed: 42}\n   - 然后 status → PENDING\n\n5. **产出中报告**：\n   - 可用数据路径和 instance 数量\n   - 是否有 LP score 数据（这是关键特征）\n   - 运行命令\n   - 预计运行时长（CPU or GPU）\n   - 任何 blockers\n\n### 注意\n- cuda:1 当前可能还有 ESJ 在跑（PID 218678）。检查 nvidia-smi 看 cuda:1 是否空闲。\n- 此实验可能 CPU-only 就够（sklearn 不需要 GPU），但如果需要 forward pass 提取 LP features 则需要 GPU。\n- 不要等结果，准备好代码 + spec 就完成。\n",
````

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_bugfix_e_theta_v3_seed_bug_20260520T171624\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"bugfix:e_theta_v3_seed_bug\", \"project\": \"struct_self_consist_ie\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-20T17:16:24.412200+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-20T17:24:51.786875+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"## 汇报\\n\\n### Root Cause\\n\\n`scierc_qwen_s123` 在 V1/V3/V4 脚本中指向 `results/exp_freeform_ablation_seed123/samples.jsonl`，但该文件的 samples 数据与 `results/exp_freeform_ablation/samples.jsonl`（seed 42）完全相同——logprobs 数组逐值一致。文件大小略有差异仅因 greedy 输出不同，但 samples（用于分析的核心数据）是 seed 42 的副本。\\n\\n### 修复\\n\\n三个脚本（`e_theta_v1.py`、`e_theta_v3_no_dataset.py`、`e_theta_v4_no_model.py`）的 `scierc_qwen_s123` 路径从：\\n- `results/exp_freeform_ablation_seed123/samples.jsonl` （重复的 seed 42 数据）\\n\\n改为：\\n- `output/exp_018_qwen_scierc_seed123/samples.jsonl` （真正的 seed 123 数据，551 instances，ID 完全匹配）\\n\\n### 修复后的 scierc_qwen 结果变化\\n\\n| Config | 指标 | 修复前 | 修复后 |\\n|--------|------|--------|--------|\\n| scierc_qwen_s42 | oracle_f1 | 0.7025 | 0.7025 (不变) |\\n| scierc_qwen_s123 | oracle_f1 | 0.7025 (= s42!) | 0.7028 (不同了) |\\n| scierc_qwen_s123 | adaptive_f1 | 0.6516 (= s42!) | 0.6481 |\\n| scierc_qwen_s123 | fixed_f1 | 0.6533 (= s42!) | 0.6456 |\\n\\nOverall 指标变化极小（adaptive_f1: 0.793164 → 0.793123），核心结论不变。\\n\\n### V1/V4 影响\\n\\nV1 和 V4 有完全相同的 bug，已同步修复和重跑。三个版本的结果 JSON 均已更新到本地 `artifacts/experiments/e_theta/` 和 `artifacts/e_theta/data/`。\\n\\n### V2 无此 bug\\n\\nV2 使用独立路径系列（`exp_001_*`），三个 SciERC seed 的 logprobs 均不同。\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```
